- **technologies-docker-container**: Containers are frequently misconfigured in production. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-docker-container))
- **technologies-elasticsearch**: Elasticsearch is notorious for misconfigured public access — billions of records have been exposed via open Elasticsearch instances.
